Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the apoptosis object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 635. The moduli space has dimension at most 298 with 1 connected components.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the apoptosis decomposition. By minimality, the element a^635 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 298-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 329. QED.
